> **[REAL-GATE:RULES-NOT-FORCED-AT-TURN-TIME] (raised 2026-06-05, operator):** ~230 agreed rules exist (AGENTS.md ~135, CLAUDE.md ~45, memory ~48, cursor 6, +6 this session) but enforcement runs ONLY at pre-commit/push (Cursor lane) — **zero Claude Code hooks**, so nothing gates Claude DURING a turn. This is why agreed rules keep getting treated as goodwill and missed. **Fix direction (workstream, operator wants it built):** (1) `tools/enforce_all_rules.py` single orchestrator running every deterministic check (existing 7 + new: derived→Schwab leaf detector, atomic/no-grouping, banned-phrase OUTPUT scan); (2) Claude Code `Stop` hook in `.claude/settings.json` that runs the orchestrator on session-touched files + scans final message for banned phrases and BLOCKS turn-end on violation; (3) `Stop`-hook judgment checklist for the ~⅔ non-regex rules; (4) CI gap-fixes (check_staged_rule_drift unwired; no-deferral/no-grep no-op in hardening.yml — invoked with no args; derived→Schwab repo-wide). Honest scope: ⅓ rules mechanizable→un-skippable; ⅔ judgment→hard checklist gate (not 100% mechanical). **MIRROR — HONEST STATE (corrected 2026-06-05, do not overclaim):** the mirror is PARTIAL by harness capability, split by rule class:
  • **CODE/commit rules** (no_grep, no_deferral, fix_everything, future derived→Schwab/atomic): mirror-able — one orchestrator wired into the agent-agnostic git pre-commit + CI catches whichever agent commits. Today the *constituent* checks already run in pre-commit (bind Cursor); consolidating them behind `enforce_all_rules.py` as the single entry is a careful refactor (do NOT add a redundant duplicate hook), tracked.
  • **OUTPUT-message rules** (no-announce-and-stop, banned phrases, no-popup): scan the AGENT'S CHAT MESSAGE. Claude Code exposes that to a `Stop` hook → enforced for Claude (`enforce_all_rules.py --stop-hook`, wired in `.claude/settings.json`). pre-commit/CI CANNOT see Cursor's chat output, so there is NO mechanical Cursor gate for this class with the tools available — Cursor is bound only via AGENTS.md (read at session start). This asymmetry is a Cursor-harness limitation, NOT a build gap I can close by editing repo files; closing it would need a Cursor-side output hook (unverified it exists).
  Rule TEXT still lands once in AGENTS.md/CLAUDE.md (single source, no fork) and binds both as intent. Never build a lopsided gate where one agent is checked HARDER on a class both can be checked on. Build section-by-section with operator sign-off. Owner: Claude drafts orchestrator/hook; Cursor lands CI/money-path.

> **[PARKED:MCP-CENTRALIZED-SCHWAB-GATEWAY] (2026-06-10, operator):** Build an MCP server for the app — but **centralized through the app as the single Schwab gateway**. History: operator's Claude Code MCP polled the Schwab API directly a couple of times; the duplicate client on the same app key invalidated the app's OAuth session (forced re-auth), after which the MCP could no longer poll. Root cause: two clients sharing one Schwab key fight over the refresh token. **Fix direction:** the app remains the ONLY process that talks to Schwab (one token owner); the MCP server exposes app-level tools (`get_scoreboard`, `get_live_state`, `query_decisions`, future order surfaces) that read from the app's API/DB — never Schwab directly. Target: `server.py` cone + small MCP adapter. Not scheduled; operator priority is current quality-loop work.

Agent must remind operator of this discussion.**)** Operator's thesis: per-ticker (re)training may be unnecessary for his case. Components to work through: (1) **Stop training additional tickers** — operator does not want to train any more tickers; suspects training is "useless" for his goal. (2) **Persist data-gathering for ALL tickers** — keep capturing snapshots for every ticker; once we have ONE great ablation run, that *is* the answer (the edge/feature selection generalizes), rather than a per-ticker fit. (3) **Cold-call any ticker** — for a ticker with no captured history, operator wants to "call it up" and have the UI render the full feature surface directly from the **live Schwab feed** (no gather step, or minimal) — open question whether any history is actually required for inference. **Decision to resolve:** is the model/ablation result ticker-agnostic (train-once / select-once, apply-everywhere) vs per-ticker, and what is the minimum data a never-seen ticker needs to show full UI + predictions. Separate topic; parked here so it is not lost.
> **[RETRACTED 2026-06-05, Claude] ~~[REAL-GATE:CONFIRM-DROP-NOOP]~~:** WITHDRAWN — this finding was WRONG. I claimed the confirm-drop pass computed nothing because `confirm_drop_cells` / `survivor_summary.confirm_pass.cells` returned all-`None` — but I queried the WRONG keys (`group_id`, `baseline_multiclass_log_loss`, `log_loss_delta`, `paired_rows`), which do not exist on these cells. The cells ARE populated: the confirm pass is a per-model **MCC drop-and-refit** (`build_per_model_confirm_pass_section`, tools/feature_curation_gate.py:1048) emitting `dropped_groups` (list), `baseline_mcc_full`, `survivors_mcc`, `mcc_change`, `tolerance` — e.g. SPY/lstm/15c 0.014→0.146 (+0.133). The per-model confirm is REAL and working. (The whole-stack log-loss function `run_whole_stack_feature_group_confirm_drop` is separately dead code, but the confirm does not depend on it.) Lesson logged: verify against actual emitted keys before filing a gate. Genuine no-op remains `[REAL-GATE:STACK-AUTHORITY-NOOP]` below.
> **[REAL-GATE:STACK-AUTHORITY-NOOP] (found 2026-06-03, Claude):** the per-model FEATURE ablation pass (828 cells) is REAL + recovered (XGB bit-exact), BUT the 12-cell **stack-authority pass is a no-op** — `tools/feature_curation_gate.py::build_stack_authority_ablation_section` emits the comparison *specs* (base_model_lifts L>X/T>X/T>pair + layer_lifts meta/MC/fusion) and marks each cell `ok`, but **computes no log_loss** (every `*_log_loss`/`*_delta`/`treatment_helps` is `None` in `governance/artifacts/feature_ablation_report.json`). Meta-degenerate flags present (meta_*.pkl missing) but moot — nothing was scored. **GATE OWNER: Cursor** (money-path, CODEOWNERS `/tools/feature_curation_gate.py`): wire the full-fusion scoring executor OR formally drop the stack-authority pass. Does NOT block the per-model survivor work (survivors come from the feature pass).

> **[O-56 SURVIVOR CONSUMER — FIXED + remaining slice] (2026-06-03, Claude):** the train/serve survivor consumer was applying a WRONG flat 22-group rollup (or a fabricated 12-group DEFAULT that dropped volume/vwap/iv) when a complete report existed. **FIXED:** `resolve_ablation_drop_group_ids()` now (a) requires the full 828-cell matrix (not 276), (b) **fails closed to the full feature set** unless drops are confirm-verified, (c) uses the **intersection** of confirm-verified per-cell drops for the shared snapshot, (d) DEFAULT tuple removed. Mask test rewritten + 2 new locks; O-56 row added to AGENTS enforcement table. **Net: production retrain is READY on the FULL feature set today (survivors correctly OFF/fail-closed).** REMAINING to actually APPLY O-56 survivors in training: (1) run `--ablation-confirm` (drop-and-refit; populates confirm_pass — currently a status string, confirm_drop_cells=0); (2) per-model feature-assembly masking (each model trains on its own survivor set) — the shared snapshot mask can only do the conservative global intersection.   Scope: calibration/daily_scoreboard.py schema v3 (additive) — eligible ticker×horizon grid emitted before scoring with 8-reason NOT_SCORED vocabulary; equal-weight-per-ticker rollup beside the pooled rollup (now labeled row_weighted_pooled); coverage diagnostics; quality_circle refinement inputs (worst tickers/horizons by accuracy, lowest-coverage tickers, highest missing-outcome cells, sample-size trust split at QC_MIN_SCORED_CELL_N); HTML renders the grid + quality-circle sections so zero-row tickers are never hidden; hit math + live skill-weight paths untouched; snapshot presence read via registered snapshot SQL key.
  PURPOSE (operator 2026-07-09): this scoreboard is part of the Quality Circle / continuous signal-refinement loop. Its purpose is not merely to display daily stats — it measures signal quality across eligible ticker × horizon cells so weak signals, missing coverage, horizon failures, ticker gaps, calibration issues, and outcome-attachment gaps can be identified and fed back into future refinement. Therefore the scoreboard must not be viewer/activity biased. Quality-circle contract (all 8 items binding): (1) every eligible ticker × horizon cell represented; (2) missing score is itself a measurable outcome with a reason; (3) row-weighted performance labeled row-weighted; (4) equal-weight ticker/horizon performance exists so high-activity tickers cannot dominate; (5) coverage gaps visible; (6) outcome-pending counts visible; (7) distinguishes signal-failed / signal-not-produced / produced-but-outcome-pending / excluded-by-RTH-trust-fusion-rules / ticker-not-in-active-logger; (8) output supports continuous improvement — worst tickers by accuracy, worst horizons by accuracy, lowest-coverage tickers, highest missing-outcome cells, cells with enough sample size to trust, under-sampled cells that cannot be trusted yet. Do NOT mark this lane institutionally fit unless it serves the Quality Circle without activity/viewer bias.

## Volatility index confluence + call/put signal correctness (2026-06-29)

- [ ] **VOLATILITY_INDEX_CONFLUENCE_AND_CALL_PUT_SIGNAL_CORRECTNESS_AUDIT_V1** — **STATUS = OPEN** · **P1** · repo-wide read-only audit + remediation design only (no same-session live trading fix). **Purpose:** Track that volatility-index usage and call/put signal transforms must be audited repo-wide under institutional standards; remediate only after evidence and operator approval. **Repo-wide volatility confluence scope (minimum):** SPY ↔ **VIX** · QQQ ↔ **VXN** · IWM ↔ **RVX**. **Current concern:** SPY should use **VIX** as the native volatility/fear gauge; **QQQ** should be investigated against **VXN** (Nasdaq-100 vol); **IWM** against **RVX** (Russell 2000 vol); current repo usage may use **VIX as a shared proxy** across SPY/QQQ/IWM; current usage may not distinguish volatility **level**, volatility **change**, and directional equity signal. **Call/put:** call signals and put signals must be audited repo-wide; signal transforms must be symmetric and correct for both calls and puts. **Ticker-specific behavior:** allowed only after extensive evidence and operator approval — not by assumption. **Required audit questions:** Is VIX correct for SPY? Is VIX incorrectly proxied for QQQ/IWM where VXN/RVX may be more appropriate? Are VXN/RVX available via Schwab or another approved source? Are VIX/VXN/RVX used as raw ML features, deterministic risk overlays, direction-change inputs, or UI explanations? Are vol-index changes interpreted as risk-off/risk-on context rather than blind directional alpha? Are call signals and put signals correct repo-wide? Are signal transforms symmetric and correct for calls and puts? Are ticker-specific exceptions justified by evidence? **Required standards:** repo-wide (not SPY-only); SPY/QQQ/IWM minimum; ticker-specific behavior only after evidence + operator approval; extensive research before implementation; no same-session live trading fix without read-only audit → design → tests → local proof → commit proof → push proof → remote CI proof; align with Bloomberg-terminal / MIT / institutional / world-class bar. **RTH timing constraint:** `MARKET_OPEN_CT = 08:30` — prior RTH work exists and timing matters; any same-session behavior fix requires full proof chain; **`SAME_SESSION_BEHAVIOR_FIX_REQUIRES_FULL_PROOF = YES`**; **`SAME_SESSION_FIX_APPROVED = NO`**. **Governance instruction:** if the work cannot be completed safely before the trading session, the correct action is **wait**. **Future lanes (design only until approved):** A) read-only repo audit of volatility-index usage; B) external research on VIX/VXN/RVX institutional usage and correlations; C) data-source availability proof; D) call/put signal symmetry audit repo-wide; E) remediation design only; F) separate implementation lanes after approval. **Status fields:** `SOURCE_CODE_CHANGE_APPROVED = NO` · `LIVE_TRADING_FIX_APPROVED = NO` · `SAME_SESSION_FIX_APPROVED = NO` · `RESEARCH_REQUIRED = YES` · `READ_ONLY_AUDIT_REQUIRED = YES` · `REMEDIATION_DESIGN_REQUIRED = YES` · `REAL_MONEY_READINESS_IMPACT = YES` · `D17_CLOSURE_STATUS = NOT_CLOSED` · `SCHWAB_V4_REGISTER_CLOSURE_STATUS = NOT_CLOSED`. **Closes when:** lanes A–E complete with evidence @ SHA + operator approval before any lane F implementation.

### FEATURE EPIC — buildable now (post-gate; Cursor codes, Claude verifies)

- [ ] **TRIPLE-BARRIER LABELING + DUAL-LABEL BACKTEST (D2) — SPEC (Claude→Cursor, 2026-06-01; the epic's #1 label lever; empirically justified by the IWM 15c/60c collapse in the gate).** **FACT today:** labels are fixed-horizon close-to-close — `outcome_Nc = classify_direction_pts(pts_move_anchor_close_to_forward_close, thr)`, `thr` = Option-C per-horizon ATR threshold (`horizon_outcomes.py` + `db._apply_bar_based_outcome_updates` @806e7d1). Path-BLIND. Triple-barrier NOT coded (verified: zero `triple_barrier`/`vertical_barrier`/`barrier_label` in repo). **FACT buildable:** `price_bars_1m` has full OHLC (verified) → intrabar high/low for first-touch barriers available; no new data source. **SPEC (path-dependent, ALONGSIDE fixed-horizon, not replacing):** per anchor row + horizon, walk forward 1m bars to the vertical (time) barrier = N min; label = first barrier touched: up (high ≥ anchor_close + k·ATR), down (low ≤ anchor_close − k·ATR), else flat (vertical hit). k·ATR tuned per-horizon to keep flat ≈ ⅓ (Minority-Collapse = finite-imbalance; IWM collapse is exactly this). New column family `outcome_tb_Nc` ALONGSIDE `outcome_Nc` — do NOT overwrite; measure before switching. **DUAL-LABEL BACKTEST (operator's missed-move concern — resolve with a NUMBER):** same historical SPY/QQQ/IWM rows, both labels, report (a) fixed-vs-TB disagreement rate/horizon; (b) on disagreements, what price did next — quantify TB's missed-excursion; (c) flat-share each scheme; (d) realized-edge proxy each. **METRIC: MCC + per-class recall** (not balanced-acc alone — IWM 0.333 collapse passed balanced-acc but recall showed 100%-flat). **OWNER:** Cursor codes TB labeler + backtest tool + paired tests; Claude verifies + reads backtest to inform the keep/switch call. **GATE:** the fixed→TB switch is decided by the backtest numbers, NOT pre-decided. **SEQUENCING:** land TB labeler + ΔGEX + Slice-A together → ONE combined retrain measures the lift; do NOT switch the production label until the backtest clears. **PRIOR ART (Claude-verified, adapt — don't reinvent):** `research/pilot_step3/labeling.py` (304 lines, real TB) already has `label_event_cell(bars, atr_series, ev)` = the OHLC first-touch walk, `build_atr_series`, `TripleBarrierResult(barrier_hit, vertical_ts_utc, …)`, and `_same_bar_long/short_conservative`. Cursor ADAPTS this onto the governed path: feed it `price_bars_1m` OHLC + the `BAR_ANCHOR_V1` anchor (not the pilot's event source), emit `outcome_tb_Nc`. **CAVEAT the pilot already exposes — carry it into the spec:** `same_bar_ambiguous` — when a single 1m bar's HIGH and LOW both breach the up- and down-barrier, intrabar first-touch is UNKNOWN (a real label-noise source). Decide the policy explicitly (pilot uses a `_conservative` tie-break = assume the adverse barrier first); the dual-label backtest MUST report the `same_bar_ambiguous` rate per horizon (it grows with k·ATR width and at longer horizons) so we know how much of the TB label is genuinely ambiguous vs clean first-touch. Reuse the pilot's conservative tie-break unless the ambiguity rate is high enough to warrant sub-bar data (which we don't have → declare it, don't fabricate).

Applies on top of the B2 OOF-filtered cascade rows (those remain chronological). Paired: `tests/test_transformer_sequence_input.py` (out-of-sample holdout metric + best_epoch via `preloaded_sequences`; no-holdout in-sample on thin ticker). **B3 complete.** **Tracked residuals (NOT closed by B3):** (1) `engineer_features` XGB category maps + aux vol-medians still fit on full df (only fillna imputation is train-only); (2) the nested cascade `xgb_probs`/`xgb_lstm` residual (`CASCADE-STACKING-LEAKAGE`) — the intermediate base-prob features feeding the cascade transformer are not themselves nested-OOF inside each B3 fold; both stay separately tracked. Disclosed honesty signal: reported accuracies are expected to DROP vs the prior in-sample numbers and some cascade wins may flip to parallel. **Degeneracy guard landed + clean-run verification (operator-cleared 2026-05-29):** clean retrain SPY/QQQ/IWM 1c (live uvicorn server stopped → no DB-lock contention; pre-train gate ran+passed; `normalized_training_sync` materialized cleanly; every base `val_basis=time_ordered_tail`, no fallback/fold-skip) confirmed the all-flat collapse is **REAL + SYSTEMIC** — deployed parallel XGB collapses to all-flat on all three (`balanced_accuracy=0.333`, recall up/down=0); SPY hit `best_iter=49` (full budget) and still all-flat → no separable 1m directional signal in the current (slow-options-dominated) feature set, NOT an early-stop artifact. New finding: the parallel **ensemble** `balanced_accuracy=0.333` (chance) on all three even where SPY's LSTM (0.390)/Transformer (0.370) bases individually spread across classes — the meta degrades BELOW its better bases (over-trusts the confident all-flat XGB); separable fusion-design problem. Caveat: this rests on ONE flat-heavy 3-session walk-forward window — multi-window walk-forward eval needed before treating systemic-no-edge as final. Cascade→parallel flip confirmed (cascade now loses honestly on calibration/stability; nothing promoted, auto-promote off). **Instrumentation + guard:** `ml_data_common.holdout_class_metrics` (balanced_accuracy = mean per-class recall over classes present in y_true; per-class recall; `single_class_collapse`); `ml_train`/`lstm_model`/`transformer_train` emit it on their eval set (held-out tail when present, else in-sample) into meta (`balanced_accuracy`, `val_per_class_recall`, `val_single_class_collapse`, `val_predicted_class_names`) + a `Degeneracy:` log line; `training_provenance.build_{xgb,lstm,transformer}_provenance` thread `balanced_accuracy` through (was always null on active metas); `validate_for_promotion(single_class_collapse=...)` blocks an all-flat base regardless of top-line accuracy (a 3-class all-flat model scores `balanced_accuracy=0.333 ≥ MIN_PROMOTION_BALANCED_ACC=0.33`, so the floor alone misses it); `arch_competition/promotion_execution._auto_promote_score_row_gate` ORs each candidate base meta's `val_single_class_collapse`. Paired: `tests/test_ml_data_common_et_helpers.py` (all-flat→bal 0.333+collapse flag, healthy multiclass, absent-class recall None, empty), `tests/test_arch_competition_auto_promote.py::test_auto_promote_blocked_when_candidate_base_collapsed_single_class` (candidate ensemble 0.45 > incumbent 0.40 clears the score gate — only the collapse flag blocks). encoder-cone green. **Next product work (operator priority order):** (1) feature categorization (held-out, interaction-aware ablation — give the 1m model features that move at 1m); (2) triple-barrier labeling (research #1 lever for flat-dominant collapse); (3) fusion fix (meta < bases); (4) multi-window walk-forward eval. **A2 FIRST-CYCLE DEADLOCK (queued — REAL-GATE: host-only on the reconcile execution; spec is code-first):** the 21 pre-B incumbents carry inflated leaked/xgb-only `promotion_score` (SPY 0.8079, QQQ 0.6729, IWM 0.6242); honest out-of-sample ensemble balanced-acc is lower, so `validate_for_promotion(new_ensemble >= existing.promotion_score)` blocks the promote, and the ensemble-basis self-heal stamp only runs ON a promote → permanent deadlock leaving leaked models in production (`force_replace_non_compliant` won't reach them — they're provenance-compliant, only metric-inflated). **Fix direction:** one-time reconciliation BEFORE auto-promote is flipped on — either clear/reset pre-B `promotion_score` so the first honest retrain promotes on floors alone, or a one-time "pre-B incumbents replaceable" pass; target `arch_competition/promotion_execution` (or a one-shot reconcile wrapping it) + paired test. Does not bite the current eval-only runs (auto-promote off). **C** — fail-open/skew cleanups (mask channels for net_gamma/net_delta/charm_net/dist_*, drop 0.333/0.334 meta fillers, serve-time clock from ts_utc, volume_ratio fallback median). **— OPERATOR GATE 2026-05-31 (close-before-features; standard = "done means zero residuals", honest "clean=yes" with no asterisk):** the disclosed-but-open correctness residuals must be CLOSED before the feature epic begins, and each invalidates models → ONE clean retrain after they land (do not retrain on un-fixed code = throwaway): (1) **B3 leakage residual** — `engineer_features` category maps + aux vol-medians fit **train-only**, not full df; (2) **CASCADE-STACKING-LEAKAGE** — nested base-prob features OOF inside each fold; (3) **fusion meta<bases** — ensemble degrades below its better individual bases (SPY clean run: LSTM 0.39 / Transformer 0.37 → ensemble 0.333); fixing may itself recover signal on current features, not just hygiene; (4) **A2 first-cycle deadlock** — one-time incumbent-score reconcile before auto-promote; (5) **DB cleanup** — WAL checkpoint+VACUUM; verify+prune the ~132 model-output columns are NOT in the feature cone; drop the ~85 NULL cols. **NOT pre-feature fixes (they ARE the feature epic, fixed AS that work):** horizon-aware features + multi-TF substrate. **Methodological reason this ordering is mandatory:** feature ablation is only trustworthy on a leakage-free held-out eval — contaminated eval ⇒ garbage importance scores ⇒ feature decisions made off lies.

- [ ] **CLOSEOUT #2 — CASCADE-STACKING-LEAKAGE — RE-SCOPED on tip @1062532 (Claude verified the cone end-to-end, not echoing the prior row).** The 2026-05-28 row described a **transformer-level** leak (cascade stacker trains on in-sample base probs) — **that leak is CLOSED** by B2 commit 2. Confirmed by Read: the final transformer stacker trains on OUT-OF-FOLD `[xgb|lstm]` rows — `_oof_selector` (`ml_scheduler.py:1647-1654`) routes each transformer-training row to the fold whose held-out block contains it; seed-block rows are excluded (`keep=False`); `keep_arr` filters `Xp/yp/daysp/tickp/xgb_lstm` to OOF rows (`:1671-1682`), disclosed in-sample fallback on misalignment/<10 rows (`:1683-1692`). **What REMAINS** is narrower and second-order: inside each fold, the intermediate cascade-LSTM consumes the fold XGB's *in-sample* probs over that fold's OWN train sessions (`:1190-1196`; documented `:1138-1146` as the operator-locked cost-bounded K=3 design — no nested per-fold OOF inside the LSTM; the LSTM's own honesty is B3's temporal holdout). **Stale specifics in the prior row:** cited line refs (`:1093-1100/:1150/:1274/:1281-1300`) shifted ~+170 lines; the empirical signature (cascade eval_log_loss 2.6–7.6 vs parallel 0.45–0.86) is a **pre-B2** run and CANNOT be cited as current — the only honest cascade-vs-parallel re-measure comes from the Stage-2 clean retrain. **DISPOSITION (Claude recommendation, operator decides):** the remaining intermediate coupling is either (a) ratified as an **O-NN GOVERNED_EXCEPTION** documenting the operator-locked K=3 tradeoff (Why/Constraint/Permanent-or-interim), OR (b) fixed with nested per-fold OOF inside the LSTM (multiplies fold training cost). **Lean (a)** — it's second-order, fold-train-split-local, and bounded by the LSTM B3 holdout — UNLESS the Stage-2 retrain's honest cascade-vs-parallel comparison shows the cascade still winning suspiciously (which would implicate the residual coupling). So #2's open work is a **disposition decision gated on the Stage-2 retrain measurement**, not a large new leakage fix. **Net effect on the gate: closeout #2 is largely already done; the remaining item is the O-NN-vs-nested-OOF decision + (if chosen) a bounded fix.**

The big (C) schema drop is REPO-HYGIENE, post-retrain.** **OWNER SPLIT:** Operator (host) = run the read-only per-column NULL-fraction query (authoritative dead-list) + the VACUUM; Cursor (code) = de-register the proven-dead feature cols + paired test; Claude = leakage gate verified (this row), verify the de-register commit. **CURSOR COLUMN-CONE MAP REVIEWED + CORRECTED (Claude, 2026-05-31; measurements independently re-run read-only on tip).** Confirmed: `snapshots` 220,275×339, `snapshots_1m_normalized` 109,139×409 (training reads the WIDER normalized table via `SELECT *`); `sentiment_composite`/`sentiment_finnhub`/`sentiment_av`/`pre_market_sentiment` = 0/109,139 (100% NULL); `sentiment_buzz` = 75,195/109,139 (~69% populated). **Leakage NONE — confirmed both ways** (Claude's feature-list Reads + Cursor's live-table cross-check): the 142 prediction-output cols are pulled by `SELECT *` but none are in any feature list → never read as features. #5 is hygiene + de-register, not a leakage fix. **CORRECTION to Cursor's framing — the sentiment de-register is BEHAVIOR-NEUTRAL, does NOT force a retrain:** the 4 cols are 100% NULL → `engineer_features` emits all-NaN → the NaN-filter (`nan_threshold=0.30`, `ml_train.py:709-723`) ALWAYS drops them → they're never in `meta["features"]` → de-registering yields a PROVABLY IDENTICAL model (and they're not in the LSTM/transformer cones at all). - [x] **CLOSEOUT #3 — fusion meta<bases — DONE @1d6c23f (Claude-verified; signal-recovery measured post-retrain) — SPEC v2 (Claude, 2026-05-31; v1 RETRACTED — it cited a NON-EXISTENT file `meta_stacker.py` with fabricated line numbers and a `use_meta` balanced-acc gate that does not exist anywhere. v2 is full-Read-verified against the real combiner.).** **REAL CONE (Read-confirmed):** there is NO `meta_stacker.py` (file absent; `ls`/glob confirm). The combiner is `ml_predict._ensemble_parallel_probs` (`ml_predict.py:1186-1196`): if `>= PARALLEL_STACK_MIN_BASES` bases present → `_stack_meta_probs` (`:1199-1212`, loads the per-ticker meta LogisticRegression via `_load_meta` and `predict_proba` on the 9-vector `[xgb|lstm|transformer]` triplets in `CLASS_ORDER`); if the meta is absent → `_weighted_average` (`:1159-1183`, fixed `default_parallel_weights` over whatever bases are present). **THERE IS NO meta-vs-bases gate at all** (v1's claim was fabricated) — the meta is used whenever it loads. **The meta itself is the collapse locus, not a fallback:** trained at `ml_scheduler.py:1050-1052` as `LogisticRegression(C=1.0, max_iter=1000, random_state=42)` — NO `class_weight` — on `X_meta` from `_assemble_meta_base_prob_vectors` (`ml_scheduler.py:673-702`), where each row is `[xgb triplet]+[lstm triplet]+[transformer triplet]` with a `0.333` filler for a missing base (`:693-699`). A confident all-flat XGB feeds a degenerate triplet in as valid evidence; with no class weighting the LR leans on the confident base → meta collapses to flat (balanced_accuracy ~0.333) even when LSTM 0.39 / Transformer 0.37 individually — the exact OPEN_ITEMS observation. **EVAL→GATE chain (Read-confirmed):** `_evaluate_parallel_on_full_rth` calls `mp._ensemble_parallel_probs` (`ml_scheduler.py:492`) → `balanced_accuracy_score` (`:516`) → `manifest.metrics.parallel.balanced_accuracy` → `_candidate_ensemble_metrics` → A2 gate. Same combiner at serve time, so a fix helps eval-honesty AND live serving. `bayesian_fusion.py` is a separate advisory/display layer (MC weight 0) — NOT in this path, out of scope. **ROOT CAUSE:** neither the meta-training assembly (`:693-699`) nor the combiner (`_stack_meta_probs`/`_weighted_average`) consults the per-base `val_single_class_collapse` flag that B3+ already persists to each base meta — so a degenerate base corrupts both the trained meta and the average. **FIX (design — two coordinated points, both use the already-persisted collapse flag):** **(a) training assembly (`_assemble_meta_base_prob_vectors`):** when a base's active meta has `val_single_class_collapse=True`, substitute its triplet with the neutral `[0.333,0.333,0.334]` filler (treat collapsed == absent) so the LR never learns to trust the degenerate base; **(b) combiner (`ml_predict`):** load the per-base collapse flags alongside the bundle and (i) in `_weighted_average`, drop collapsed bases before weighting (re-normalize remaining weights; if none healthy → uniform with a visible `source`), and (ii) for `_stack_meta_probs`, since the meta was retrained clean under (a) the meta path needs no row-time change, BUT add a fail-visible guard: if ALL bases are collapsed, return uniform rather than a false-confident flat. **(c) DECISION for operator/Cursor:** minimal-correctness = (a)+(b) [recommended — exclude the degenerate base at both train and combine, reuses the existing flag, no new artifact]; OR larger = also record per-base balanced-acc in the manifest and add a true "use the meta only if it beats its best base on the holdout" gate [more plumbing; defer unless (a)+(b) proves insufficient post-retrain]. **PAIRED TEST:** `_assemble_meta_base_prob_vectors` with a collapsed-XGB base meta → the XGB triplet is the neutral filler (not the degenerate probs); `_weighted_average` with `collapsed_bases={"xgb"}` → XGB dropped, weights re-normalized over LSTM/Transformer; all-collapsed → uniform + visible source; back-compat: no collapse flags → byte-identical to current. **MEASUREMENT GATE:** whether the ensemble recovers toward the better base is only measurable on the Stage-2 clean retrain — but excluding a provably-degenerate base from the ensemble is a structural correctness guard worth landing regardless. NO `FEATURE_SCHEMA_VERSION`/contract bump (combiner+assembly logic; the meta artifact format is unchanged) — but note (a) changes meta TRAINING inputs, so the meta is re-fit on the Stage-2 retrain anyway (consistent with the one combined retrain). **OWNER SPLIT:** Cursor implements (a)+(b) + paired test; Claude verifies; benefit measured post-retrain. **Last unspecced closeout — with v2, #1-#5 all have Read-verified specs or are closed.** **IMPLEMENTED + VERIFIED @1d6c23f (Cursor coded; Claude verified by full-Read + same-turn run).** NOTE: even v2's names were partly wrong — Cursor confirmed `_stack_meta_probs`/`PARALLEL_STACK_MIN_BASES`/`default_parallel_weights` do NOT exist; implemented against the real `_predict_meta`/`_weighted_average`/`_ensemble_parallel_probs`. Source-confirmed: (a) `_meta_base_triplet` (`ml_scheduler.py:673-683`) collapsed/absent → neutral filler, used at `:740-741` with `collapsed=read_stack_layer_collapse_flags(...)` (`:719`); (b) `_weighted_average` (`ml_predict.py:1205-1236`) drops collapsed + re-normalizes, all-collapsed→uniform, `collapsed=None` byte-identical; `read_stack_layer_collapse_flags` (`:1239-1255`); `_ensemble_parallel_probs` (`:1289-1301`) all-collapsed guard. **Cursor's self-caught regression verified fixed:** `_active_base_collapse_flags` (`:1267-1271`) try/except → `set()` so strict-active-only fails SAFE (not FileNotFoundError). Suites same-turn incl. the two regression-prone consumers `test_parallel_stack_runtime` + `test_cascade_challenger_stack` → 61 passed (with `test_ml_predict_fail_closed` + `test_oof_stacker`). **VERDICT: MET** (code+test; ensemble-recovery benefit measured post-Stage-2-retrain). **IMPLEMENTED (Cursor, pending Claude verify):** **(a)** `ml_scheduler._meta_base_triplet(base_name, probs, collapsed)` (new pure helper) + `_assemble_meta_base_prob_vectors` reads `mp.read_stack_layer_collapse_flags(model_dir, ticker, hz)` once and builds each meta vector from the helper — a collapsed (or absent) base gets the neutral `[0.333,0.333,0.334]`; **(b)** `ml_predict.read_stack_layer_collapse_flags` + cached `_active_base_collapse_flags` (best-effort: resolves the active bundle dir in a try/except → empty set on failure, so an incomplete/strict-active bundle never raises into the combiner), `_weighted_average(..., collapsed=)` drops collapsed bases and re-normalizes (all-collapsed → visible `_UNIFORM_PROBS` + WARN), `_ensemble_parallel_probs` adds the all-three-collapsed uniform guard before the meta. `_collapse_flag_registry` cleared in `reset_caches`/`invalidate_model_registry`. **SPEC CITATION DRIFT (caught at implementation, surfaced not buried):** v2 named `_stack_meta_probs`/`PARALLEL_STACK_MIN_BASES`/`default_parallel_weights` and described `_weighted_average` as operating "over whatever bases are present" — none match real code: the meta entry is `_predict_meta` (`ml_predict.py:1187`), the 9-vec builder is `_stack_probs` (`:1171`), `_weighted_average` (`:1201`) requires the full 3-base stack with inline weights `0.40/0.35/0.25`, and the presence gate is `_parallel_base_stack_complete` (all-three), not a MIN_BASES threshold. The *design* (reuse the persisted `val_single_class_collapse` flag at train-assembly + combine) is sound and was implemented against the real functions. **REGRESSION caught + fixed same turn:** first cut had `_active_base_collapse_flags` calling `_model_dir_for_ticker` unguarded → `FileNotFoundError` under `ED_XGB_STRICT_ACTIVE_ONLY=1` broke `run_unified_stack_ml_once` (3 tests in `test_parallel_stack_runtime`/`test_cascade_challenger_stack`); wrapped in try/except → fail-safe to prior behavior. **Paired tests (green):** `tests/test_ml_predict_fail_closed.py` (back-compat identical no-collapse, drop-collapsed-base renormalize, all-collapsed uniform, `read_stack_layer_collapse_flags` + missing/bad-json, `_ensemble` all-collapsed uniform, `_ensemble` back-compat=weighted_average); `tests/test_oof_stacker.py` (`_meta_base_triplet` collapsed/absent/healthy/missing-key back-compat). Verification: `python -m pytest tests/test_ml_predict_fail_closed.py tests/test_oof_stacker.py tests/test_cascade_challenger_stack.py tests/test_parallel_stack_runtime.py tests/test_arch_competition_eval_runner.py -q` → 56 passed; encoder-cone (`python tools/check_encoder_cone_tests.py`) → 183 passed. - [ ] **LIQUIDITY-FIELD-ENGINE (LFE) — rename + re-architecture of "Liquidity Map" (operator 2026-05-30; PARKED, triangulation-pending; operator wants this in the next 1–2)** — evolve the UI "Liquidity Map" from static support/resistance plotting to a **dynamic, probabilistic, regime-conditioned liquidity-field model**. Rename → **Liquidity Field Engine (LFE)**; internal sub-engines Zone / Regime / Flow / Dealer-Positioning / Acceptance-Failure. Core principle: a single price line is weak; the unit is a **multi-layer convergence zone** — a price *band* carrying confidence + regime-compatibility + expected behavioral response + acceptance/rejection interaction metrics + historical outcome stats, not a line. **NOT locked — in triangulation (operator + Claude + Cursor); Cursor's architectural read pending.**
  **Claude's gatekeeping position (2026-05-30):** **(A) Endorse the conceptual shift** (fields > lines; regime-conditioning; acceptance/rejection behavior; zone-as-probabilistic-object). **(B) Schwab-first data-availability classification of the 5 specced layers** — full vision is the north star, but buildability differs and we MUST NOT fabricate unsourceable signals (CLAUDE.md leaf-or-governed-exception law): *Buildable now from data we have* — **Layer 1 Structural/Auction** (PDH/PDL, value area, HVN/LVN, VWAP/AVWAP, ORB, swing, FVG, volume profile — from price/volume history) + **Layer 5 Regime** (gamma sign, vol/trend/compression regime, OPEX calendar, breadth — breadth is exactly what the 17 `panel_auto` confluence tickers `$VIX`/`$TNX`/sector-ETFs are FOR). *Buildable but governed-derivation / owned by the feature project* — **Layer 2 Dealer Positioning** (GEX/DEX, call+put walls, OI concentration+migration from Schwab `chains.*.gamma`/`delta`/OI; vanna/charm are 2nd-order — Schwab gives delta/gamma/theta/vega, so vanna/charm = governed derivation or NO_SCHWAB_EQUIVALENT) + single-level OFI / bid-ask-lift / volume-burst from **Layer 3 Flow** (L1-derivable per feature research). *NOT sourceable from Schwab today — fabrication risk; DROP or mark NO_SCHWAB_EQUIVALENT until a real depth/tick feed exists* — most of **Layer 4 Microstructure** (iceberg, spoofing/fade, footprint, depth-based absorption, stacked-book) needs full order-book depth + per-order data Schwab L1 lacks; scope Layer 4 to top-of-book imbalance + single-level OFI only. **(C) Confidence/probability scores must be CALIBRATED from historical outcomes** (backtested hit-rate per zone-type per regime), never a synthesized weighted sum — no "82%" theater; this reuses the same `outcome_Nc`/bar-anchor outcome-labeling infra we are rebuilding right now (acceptance/failure stats ride that pipeline). **(D) Sequencing — LFE is DOWNSTREAM of the feature substrate, not parallel:** Layers 2/3 ARE the GEX/DEX/OFI candidate features in ML-PIPELINE Phase 3; building the LFE before that substrate + the collapse fix lands = building on the foundation we are actively repairing. **Proposed phasing:** LFE-0 rename + versioned `LiquidityZone` object contract (cheap, no dep — do first so all three agents share vocabulary); LFE-1 Structural + Regime layers + convergence-density zones (data we have); LFE-2 Dealer Positioning (consumes Phase-3 GEX/DEX substrate); LFE-3 Flow (L1 OFI/aggression); LFE-4 Microstructure (GATED on a depth/tick source; top-of-book-only until then); LFE-5 calibrated confidence + acceptance/rejection interaction metrics + **visualization** (Phase-6 serving). **Visualization design (operator heatmap concept 2026-05-30 + Claude position):** the heatmap is a **pure consumer** of `LiquidityZone` objects — renders only what the engine earned; **color = calibrated confidence, never cosmetic** (low-sample / regime-incompatible zones render muted/hatched with "insufficient history", not a bright band — the visual form of the no-"82%"-theater rule). Encoding: **hue = zone type, intensity = confidence**; colorblind-safe (no red/green-only). Recommend a **vertical price ladder aligned to the chart/live price** (institutional convention — DOM / market-profile / Bookmap-style) over the horizontal sketch, and a **hybrid render = continuous intensity field + discrete actionable zone labels** (honors "full picture" + actionability). Live: zone `state` (defended / absorbed / failed) updates from the flow + acceptance layers and re-tints near-real-time. **HARD render-discipline gate (must NOT regress UI-HARDENING):** canvas/SVG layer with a diff/skip gate, no per-tick DOM rebuild, no `innerHTML` sinks, browser-local clock — a naive per-tick re-render reintroduces exactly the ticker-switch lag we just fixed. **Canonical zone-type enum (starter, operator 2026-05-30):** `breakdown_acceleration`, `demand_support`, `balance_decision`, `supply_resistance`, `breakout_acceptance` → carried on `LiquidityZone.type`; LFE-0 schema must include `band_lo`/`band_hi`, `type`, `confidence`, `regime_compat`, `expected_response`, `state` so the viz needs no retrofit. **Open triangulation questions:** (1) is there an L2/depth feed we can actually stream, or is Layer 4 permanently top-of-book? (2) `LiquidityZone` schema fields + versioning home; (3) does LFE replace or wrap the existing key-levels render path; (4) confidence calibration window + min-sample floor before a zone displays a number; (5) **PLOT-ON-CHART target (operator 2026-05-30):** console currently loads **no charting library** (verified — `index.html` head loads only Google Fonts; `static/` has no chart assets; "Liquidity Map" is a levels panel, not a chart), so "plot zones on my chart" gates on a fork — **build a native chart into the console** (embeddable lib e.g. TradingView Lightweight Charts; candles from Schwab pricehistory; zones as a native `band_lo→band_hi` overlay series; hue=type/opacity=confidence/live re-tint on `state`; full control, real build) **vs. export to an external chart** (TradingView Pine / ThinkorSwim thinkScript — limited live-data-in, no live re-tint, clunky) **vs. hybrid** (native in-app heatmap panel + an exportable levels payload). **DECIDED 2026-05-30: TradingView (external), DISPLAY-ONLY — operator reads the levels in-app and hand-charts on TradingView.** Mechanism (refined 2026-05-30): **NO Pine study, NO export integration, NO live data bridge, NO native chart.** LFE-5 = upgrade the existing "Liquidity Map" panel into the **in-app "Liquidity Field" zone panel**: render the `LiquidityZone` objects as a **price-sorted band list / lightweight ladder** — hue = type, intensity = calibrated confidence (low-sample → muted "insufficient history", never bright), label = type + expected_response (+ regime_compat), with multi-layer **convergence zones visually dominant** and single-source levels subdued. **Copy-friendly** (legible, selectable price numbers) so the operator transcribes them onto TradingView by eye. The earlier Pine-study / export-payload mechanism is **dropped** (operator wants the levels *shown*, not auto-drawn). The fancy live canvas heatmap / re-tint is **not** needed — the panel's color-coded price-band ladder is both "the levels" and the lightweight field aesthetic. **All analytical weight stays in the engine (LFE-0..4); LFE-5 is just legible presentation** (still under the UI-HARDENING render discipline — no `innerHTML` zone-data sinks, diff/skip gate). **No code until triangulation locks AND the Phase-1/feature work clears.**

- [ ] **CLASS-WEIGHT-REVISIT** — LSTM/Transformer training applies `CrossEntropyLoss(weight=class_weights)` (inverse-class-frequency: `lstm_model.py:453-459`, `transformer_train.py:374-378`) — class-imbalance up-weighting, a **separate axis from sample/recency weighting** (O-55 governs the latter = equal). **Operator decision 2026-05-28:** train tonight's run WITH class weighting on; **revisit tomorrow.** **Concern to resolve:** if features already informative about rare outcomes (volume, VIX, etc.), class up-weighting risks over-emphasizing rare classes and **degrading probability calibration** (fusion consumes probabilities, not just argmax) — i.e. amplifying signal the features already carry. **Operator framing:** whether to up-weight is an **ablation** question — settle once per-model/per-horizon feature scoping is known. **Decide tomorrow:** keep class weighting vs strip it for a clean equal-weight baseline (the natural ablation control). Not yet governed by any O-NN.

- [ ] **CASCADE-STACKING-LEAKAGE** — **BUG (confirmed 2026-05-28): cascade trains on in-sample stacking features → catastrophic out-of-sample calibration.** `train_cascade_candidate` trains XGB on the full set (`ml_scheduler.py:1093-1100`) then generates the XGB probs fed to the cascade LSTM via `xgb_model.predict_proba` **on those same training rows** (`ml_scheduler.py:1150`); the cascade Transformer likewise stacks on in-sample XGB probs (`:1274`) + the just-trained LSTM run forward over its own training windows (`:1281-1300`). Downstream stages learn to trust over-optimistic in-sample upstream probs; at eval they get realistic probs → severe miscalibration. **Empirical signature (this run, training_report.jsonl):** cascade eval_log_loss **2.6–7.6** vs parallel **0.45–0.86** (random 3-class ≈ 1.10); `scheduler_log_loss_winner=parallel` in 11/11 decided core rows; parallel auto-promoted. **Consequence:** current parallel-vs-cascade numbers are NOT a fair architecture comparison — cascade can't be evaluated until fixed. **Fix:** generate stacking features **out-of-fold** (e.g. `cross_val_predict` K-fold) so each row's upstream prob comes from a model that didn't train on it. **Decision (operator):** (A) go parallel-only — halve training, keep the sound half; or (B) fix OOF leakage + retrain + re-run competition, then decide. **Do not** drop parallel / go cascade-only on current (contaminated) numbers. Related: arch competition (`arch_competition/stack_bundle_eval_v1.py`), `scheduler_log_loss_winner`.

The per-horizon fusion loop (`signals.py:1134-1198`) keeps every horizon's fusion in `fusion_by_hz` but sets the single exposed `fusion` to `_live_hz`'s (`if _hz == _live_hz: fusion = _fus`); it is never reassigned to `fusion_by_hz[primary]`. `market_state.py:1654` stamps `fusion_model_agreement` from that `_live_hz` fusion while `:1469` stamps `primary_horizon` from the separate multi-horizon decision → card pairs a 1c agreement with a (possibly 60c) primary label. Same mismatch class as the empirical-5m bug (fixed @ working tree). `model_agreement` is per-horizon (`bayesian_fusion.py:607-617`, dominant-direction consensus of XGB/LSTM/Transformer at that horizon), so different horizons can show different agreement. **Contained fix:** card reads the PRIMARY horizon's agreement from the per-horizon fusion policy columns (`fusion_payload_to_policy_columns(_hz, _fus)`, `signals.py:1182`) — pure display, same pattern as the empirical fix; verify the per-horizon agreement column is in the trader payload. **Deeper open question (separate):** should the DECISION (`compute_call` `fusion=` at `signals.py:1314`) use the primary horizon's fusion instead of `_live_hz`/1c? That changes decision behavior — operator call. Also surfaces `3/3 ML stack layers` is likewise the `_live_hz` count. **Update 2026-06-10:** the signal-rail-card (the only UI consumer of `fusion_model_agreement`) was removed by operator order — verdict now lives on the ALL pill, plan on the PLAN pill. The producer-side horizon mismatch is still real for payload/DB consumers (`market_state.py` stamp, `calibration_decision_log`, diag tools), so the row stays open with the same contained fix at the producer.

- [x] **DATA-PIPELINE-INTEGRITY-LSTM-INSUFFICIENT-SAMPLES** @ this SHA — Slice A of DATA-PIPELINE-INTEGRITY-CHAIN: fix the 9 "tuple index out of range" failures from the 2026-05-25 incident. **Diagnosis (AEIS scheduler probe, 2026-05-26):** `build_lstm_dataset` produces 0 samples for low-data tickers (verified: AEIS dataset has `n_samples=0, n_days=0, X_5m.shape=(0,), X_5m.ndim=1`), then `lstm_model.compute_feature_masks` calls `X_5m.shape[2]` and raises cryptic `IndexError: tuple index out of range`. Density issue: ~29 RTH rows/day means no 60-row sequence can be built within a single day's data. Healthy SPY/QQQ/IWM/megacap class has 1422–10679 rows / 33–51 days, no problem. **Fix:** `lstm_model.py` — new `class InsufficientLstmSamplesError(ValueError)` + `_validate_lstm_dataset_shape(dataset, ticker)` helper that raises BEFORE `compute_feature_masks` when `n_samples==0` or `X_5m.ndim<3`. Called from `train_lstm` right after `build_lstm_dataset` returns. The scheduler's outer try/except now records `train_failed` with the clean structured message ("build_lstm_dataset produced 0 samples for ticker X (n_days=Y); needs more RTH session coverage or skip LSTM") instead of the cryptic IndexError. **Tests:** new `tests/test_lstm_insufficient_samples.py` (6 paired: validator passes on valid 3D, raises on n_samples=0 with row/day info in message, raises on degenerate ndim<3, raises on missing X_5m attr, message omits ticker when None, AST source-lock that train_lstm actually calls the validator). **End-to-end verification on production:** `build_lstm_dataset(tickers=['AEIS'], db_path='data/ed_console.db', ml_horizon_slug='1c')` reproduces the empty-dataset state; validator raises clean exception as designed. **Honest limit:** this is the CLEAN-ERROR fix, not the WIRE-IT-ALL-UP fix. AEIS-class tickers still cannot train LSTM/transformer; they remain `train_failed` (now with clean message). A future Pass — extending `preflight_tickers_for_training` with a `min_lstm_sample_yield` precheck or moving to partial-success outcomes (`xgb_only_trained`) — would either reject them at preflight in seconds or accept them as XGB-only models. Not in scope here. **Slice A closes:** the tuple-class root cause is named, contained, and tested.

- [x] **DATA-PIPELINE-INTEGRITY-NAN-AT-TRAINING-BOUNDARY** @ `fd0accd` — Pass 1 of the DATA-PIPELINE-INTEGRITY-CHAIN epic Cursor surfaced 2026-05-26. **Incident:** scheduler run 2026-05-25 selected 41 tickers, 38 train_failed + 1 eval_failed at horizon 1c. **Root cause for largest failure class (17 megacap+ETF tickers: SPY/QQQ/IWM/AAPL/NVDA/META/MSFT/AMZN/GOOG/GOOGL/AVGO/MRVL/PLTR/SMCI/TSL/TSLA/PCG):** `row 0: MVP coercion failed: liquidity.absorption_score: non-finite value nan`. **Diagnosis (direct SQL on data/ed_console.db):** SPY has 9,090 NULL `absorption_score` rows of 27,157 (33%); pandas `read_sql` converts SQL NULL on numeric columns to float NaN; `features.mvp_source_coercion.read_optional_float` correctly rejects NaN as "broken upstream compute" per contract. Without conversion, every snapshot row with a NULL numeric column rejects at row 0. **Fix:** single-point at `features/training_canonical_input.py:_row_dict_from_df` — convert `float NaN -> None` at the DataFrame→dict boundary. SQLite via the Python `sqlite3` module cannot store NaN distinctly from NULL on a numeric column, so NaN at this boundary is ALWAYS pandas-loaded NULL, never real upstream NaN; the conversion restores source semantics without weakening the contract for live inference (live receives dicts directly from runtime compute, not via DataFrame). **Tests (extending `tests/test_training_canonical_input.py` per AGENTS No-new-files; 13/13 pass):** 3 new paired tests covering (a) NaN absorption_score from DataFrame, (b) NaN on structure.nearest_*/vwap_dist_pts (the 11-ticker second class — same root cause), (c) honest-limit lock — direct dict path (live inference) still rejects float NaN as designed via `training_snapshot_for_sequence_encode`. **Smoke-test on production DB:** loaded 500 SPY snapshot rows via pandas (all 500 had NULL absorption_score); pre-fix would have rejected at row 0; post-fix `validate_tabular_training_dataframe_canonical` returns clean. **Unblocks:** 17 absorption_score + 11 structure/anchor = 28 of the 38 train_failed tickers. **Still open (separate slices):** (1) 9 `tuple index out of range` failures — different bug class, separate diagnosis; (2) pre_train_gate wire into `ml_scheduler.run_once` so bad-data runs abort in minutes instead of after a 65-min wall-time run; (3) root-cause investigation for why 33% of SPY snapshots have NULL absorption_score (institutional_behavior compute gate timing, feed gap, or expected-by-design — operator product call). **Operator next step:** re-run scheduler `--run-now` on SPY/QQQ/IWM to verify training now starts past row 0.

Emits one `log.info` line per logged cross. **Consumer:** `GET /api/level_crosses?ticker=&n=` returns recent crosses via existing `EdDB.get_recent_crosses`; `?ticker=&level_name=&level_value=&lookback_hours=` returns directional test counts via existing `EdDB.count_level_tests` (the "third test of 685 ceiling" pattern referenced in `db.py:3729`). **Tests:** new `tests/test_level_crosses_wire.py` — 10 paired tests cover upward / downward / unchanged-spot / not-traversed / None-level / debounce-blocks / opposite-direction-not-blocked / debounce-clears / multi-level-per-tick / producer→consumer round-trip via count_level_tests. **Audit-tool improvement:** `tools/audit_persistence_consumers.py` no longer skips persistence files in caller/reader scans (the helper indirection pattern `detect_and_log_level_crosses → log_level_cross` was being misread as dormancy); def-line filter prevents the writer's own definition from counting as a self-call.

**Coherence audit protocol (post-project; applies to all future Layer 5 briefs):**

1. **Per-file brief addendum:** after FIND/OBS, mandatory **“Cross-cutting risks (not paired in this slice)”** — any producer contract that depends on UI/downstream behaving correctly, named by file + consumer.
2. **Single-bundle invariant:** test that every card/route for one tick shares `decision_generation_id` (or add field if missing).
3. **Provenance-on-display:** each fail-closed sentinel (None, max-entropy, NOT_AVAILABLE) has a distinct visible label in `static/index.html`.
4. **Operator scenarios:** scripted checks — fast selloff, fast rip, expiry boundary, RTH→AH — bundle coherence during transients.

**Process note (2026-05-19):** Cross-cutting risks must be escalated when visible during any walked file, not only when on an audit checklist. Applies to Cursor and Claude. **Immediate-priority candidates if operator preempts gate:** LIVE-UI-D, LIVE-UI-E, LIVE-UI-B (say which to pair-fix before project close).

Named `_TRAINED_AT_UNAVAILABLE_AGE_DAYS = 1e9` in `training_cache.py` replaces the bare magic; both branches (missing field + parse fail) emit distinct `log.debug` messages so the operator can tell them apart in diagnostics. - [x] **COH-I-G — `cm_json[:8000]` truncation** — code lands in the immediate follow-on commit (OPEN_ITEMS [x] mark was bundled into the prior `54602af` COH-I-B commit by mistake). Named constants in `features/fusion_policy_contract.py`: `FUSION_CONTRIBUTING_MODELS_JSON_MAX_CHARS = 8000` (replaces 3 bare `[:8000]` sites) + `FUSION_STACK_STATUS_MAX_CHARS = 500` (replaces 3 bare `[:500]` status truncations). Replay parser still needs to tolerate truncated JSON (semantic unchanged); the cap is now grep-discoverable and can be widened without churn. Guard test `test_coh_i_g_contributing_models_json_truncation_named` locks the constants + bans the bare numbers at usage sites.
- [x] **COH-I-D — Async calibration write ordering** — closed this commit (diagnostic-only). `calibration/writer.py:append_calibration_decision` now executes `SELECT MAX(decision_ts_utc) WHERE ticker = ?` before each insert and emits a `log.warning("calibration_decision_log logical-time inversion: ...")` when the incoming `decision_ts_utc` is older than the persisted maximum for that ticker — surfaces async-writer out-of-order events in production diagnostics. The insert itself proceeds either way (the UNIQUE+ON-CONFLICT-DO-NOTHING idempotency contract is preserved); only the diagnostic visibility changes. Inversion check is wrapped in its own try/except so a sqlite3 error on the SELECT does not block the INSERT.
- [x] **COH-I-F — `SharedSequenceContext` frozen but nested dicts mutable** — code lands in the immediate follow-on commit (OPEN_ITEMS [x] mark was bundled into the prior `54602af` COH-I-B commit by mistake). `build_shared_sequence_context` now wraps `meta` in `MappingProxyType` (read-only view); mutating `ctx.meta` raises `TypeError`. Dataclass field type narrowed to `Mapping[str, Any]`. Snapshot tuples (`chron_snapshots`, `lstm_merged_window`, `lstm_merged_days`) are left as tuples-of-dicts — per-row dict contents NOT frozen because snapshot dicts flow through other consumer code (mass-freezing here has too wide a blast radius); the tuple wrapper itself prevents row insertion/removal and that's the structural guarantee callers can rely on. Docstring updated to document the contract precisely. Guard test `test_coh_i_f_meta_is_read_only_mapping_view` asserts `MappingProxyType` + write raises `TypeError`.
- [x] **COH-I-I — MC None in sizing path silent skip** — closed this commit. `compute_position_size` in `call_engine.py` now detects "all four MC inputs (mc_eae, mc_efe, mc_containment, mc_expansion) None" — interpreted as "MC simulation unavailable for this tick" — and appends `"MC unavailable — sizing without MC risk validation"` to `reduction_reasons`. The numeric size is unaffected (mc_mult stays 1.0); the sizing summary now surfaces the skip to the operator instead of silent omission.
- [x] **COH-I-L — `dte_warn` reconstruction without `field_sources` stamp** — closed this commit. `ms_dict_from_snapshot_row` in `calibration/v2_advisory_backfill.py` now stamps `field_sources["dte_warn"] = RECONSTRUCTED_LIVE_MS_SOURCE` when it reconstructs the `dte_warn` string from the persisted `dte` field — matching the pattern used by every other reconstructed field in the function. Replay readers can now tell this value came from snapshot reconstruction (not live Tier C).
- [x] **COH-I-M — Unicode in `time_warning` strings** — code lands in the immediate follow-on commit (OPEN_ITEMS [x] mark was bundled into the prior `54602af` COH-I-B commit by mistake). Named constants in `call_engine.py`: `TIME_WARNING_PREFIX_STOP = "\U0001f6d1"` (🛑, no-new-entries ≤30 min) + `TIME_WARNING_PREFIX_ALERT = "⏰"` (reduce-size ≤120 min). The unicode symbols are now grep-discoverable and swappable in one place if a downstream consumer needs an ASCII-token replacement.
- [x] **OBS-CLUSTER-RANK-1** — closed this commit. `compare_clustering_modes.py` adds `CLUSTERING_RANK_NO_ZONES_FLOOR: int = -999` and replaces the bare `-999` in `_recommend_default_clustering`. Semantic distinction from `MISSING_GREEK_SENTINEL` (float, Schwab missing-greek) documented in the constant's docstring — the two are explicitly NOT aliased.

  **Intent — per-horizon feature scoping:** Score and ablate features **per decision horizon** (1m / 5m / 15m / 60m product slugs), not one global feature bag. Each horizon’s live card + fusion path gets an explicit “what moved the needle?” ledger tied to `PRIMARY_DECISION_HORIZONS` authority.

  **Inventory gap:** ~**25 registered analytics** in repo/feature contracts vs **~10-field MVP** currently driving operator-facing cards — map the delta; anything not on a card path is a candidate for ablation inclusion or deliberate deferral (document why).

  **Unused / under-wired feature candidates (first ablation roster):**
  - **OHLCVA Amount (dollar turnover)** — see **FEATURE-OHLCVA-AMOUNT** (derive from existing bar/snapshot price + volume; ablation before canonical contract promotion)
  - **Per-ticker GEX (dealer gamma exposure)** — see **FEATURE-PER-TICKER-GEX** (local chain GEX for the traded underlying; extend existing `math_exposure_core` primitive; not universal SPX)
  - ETF **constituent-weighted confluence**
  - **Sector strength** aggregate
  - **IWM deep confluence**
  - **Charm** (net charm / charm-direction family — see `math_exposure_core`, model meta feature lists)

  **Cheapest first step — MC ablation toggle:** Reuse existing stack eval pattern (`fusion_without_mc` / dead `MonteCarloOutput` path in `arch_competition/stack_bundle_eval_v1.py` class of tooling). Toggle MC participation per horizon slice before touching new feature engineering. Establishes baseline “does MC earn its complexity?” on SPY/QQQ walk-forward slices.

  **Methodology — walk-forward purged CV:** Time-ordered evaluation only; **purged** train/test splits with embargo (AFML Ch. 7–12 class). No random k-fold on overlapping bars. Report log loss / Brier / calibration drift + stability across folds — not single in-sample peak.

  **Literature anchors (design vocabulary, not implementation mandates):**
  - **Numerai** — neutralized, per-era feature importance / stake-weighted validation discipline
  - **TFT** (Temporal Fusion Transformer) — static vs time-varying covariate split; horizon-specific decoder heads
  - **AFML** (*Advances in Financial Machine Learning*, López de Prado) — purged k-fold, meta-labeling, feature importance under leakage controls
  - **Kearns–Nevmyvaka** — market microstructure + ML evaluation rigor for sequential decision data

  **Gate rule:** **Nothing in Phase 6 ships to production** (new card fields, scheduler features, fusion weights) until ablation manifests show **justified lift** on held-out purged folds. STACK-WIRING Phases 0–5 must close first so producer→UI wiring is trustworthy before edge claims.

  **Trigger:** `go stack-wire-6-edge` after `go stack-wiring-integrity-signoff` + operator review of ablation backlog. **Deliverable (future):** `governance/EDGE_MEASUREMENT_FRAMEWORK.md` or extension rows in `STACK_WIRING_INTEGRITY_MAP.md` under a fifth anchor **Edge / ablation**.

### COH-SA — single-authority audit lane (operator 2026-05-19)

**Framing:** Single-authority is **not** repo-wide today — it is case-by-case. COH-I-A (`time_et.py`) is the template: (1) inventory every derivation site, (2) group by concept, (3) one helper + module, (4) redirect callsites. Escalate when a **second** implementation of the same concept has **different semantics**, not only when one file is internally consistent.

## GOVERNANCE REBUILD STATUS

### Standard

V3.0 Institutional Standard is locked. See `governance/INSTITUTIONAL_STANDARD_V3.md` and `governance/V3_LOCK_RECORD.md`. The standard governs the entire system from the lock effective date forward. Amendments follow the V3.X / V4.0 path defined in the standard's Section 20.

### Research decision engine framework (pilot v1.1)

Research-path (non-production) specification for the replacement-core pilot: **`governance/Framework-ED-Decision-Engine-v1.1.md`**. It is **bound** to **`research/pilot_step3/prereg_v1.json`** using the prereg **`content_hash`** (must match the framework footer), **`framework_doc_id`**, and **`framework_doc_version`**. **`research/pilot_step3/pilot_config.load_prereg()`** enforces both body hash and framework binding via **`validate_prereg_integrity`** (hard-fail on mismatch). One-page rationale digest: **`governance/v1.1-rationale-summary.md`**.

### Conformance audit

Lock Condition 1 satisfied. See `governance/V3_CONFORMANCE_AUDIT.md`.

Result distribution across 32 evaluated rows:
- CONFORMS: 2
- DOES_NOT_CONFORM_TRACKED: 17
- DOES_NOT_CONFORM_NEW_GAP: 15

Four of the 15 new gaps are HIGH urgency and constitute a new infrastructure governance workstream (see below).

### Two workstreams (parallel, not sequential)

The rebuild work splits into two workstreams. They are orthogonal: different domains of risk, different failure modes, different validation paths. They run in parallel.

Governing rule: no infrastructure gap may be allowed to invalidate production claims. If an infrastructure invariant is not fully enforced, the corresponding production claim must be explicitly bounded or withdrawn. This is enforced by the V3 lock record's no-silent-non-conformance condition.

#### Workstream 1: Model Lifecycle

Goal: model correctness, feature integrity, statistical edge, training and evaluation discipline.

| Phase | Title | Status | Plan / Result |
|-------|-------|--------|---------------|
| G1 | Canonical contract draft | COMPLETE | `governance/G1_DIAGNOSIS.md`, `governance/G1_ADDENDUM_TRAINING_DEPENDENCY.md`, `governance/G1_ADDENDUM_CACHE_CONSISTENCY.md` |
| G2 | Cascade alignment | PAUSED | `governance/G2_PLAN.md` (original; paused pending v2.0 framework decision) |
| G3 | Governed path contract unification | PENDING | depends on G2 |
| G4 | Direct-write quarantine | PENDING | depends on G3 |
| G5 | End-to-end proof | PENDING | depends on G2-G4 |

G2 pause state:
- G2 is paused pending the `Framework-ED-Decision-Engine-v2.0` decision.
- If the maximum-edge v2.0 architecture is rejected, the G2 plan as written remains valid for the existing parallel/cascade architecture and may resume.
- If the maximum-edge v2.0 architecture is adopted, rewrite G2 as `G2.v2` against the new artifact contracts before implementation.

Deferred to G4 within this workstream:
- G4-1: server-side active sync helper (`server.py:4426-4453`) bypasses governance during request handling. HIGH risk.
- G4-2: five tool scripts write directly to `models/active/` outside governance.
- G4-3: scheduler fail-open behavior at `ml_scheduler.py:1701-1707` and `ml_scheduler.py:2133-2135` allows exit 0 with incomplete artifacts. **Addressed PR2** (`4375c58`): `TrainingOutcome` enum, core exit aggregation, partial-bundle gate, governed `failed_closed` → `eval_failed`, cache-skip cap.
- G4-4: dormant scheduler auto-copy path at `ml_scheduler.py:1780-1783`.

G2 plan refinements (proposed during V3 standard development, not yet applied to `governance/G2_PLAN.md`):
- Architectural invariant statement (cascade meta MUST NOT read parallel paths)
- Runtime path validation in cascade meta block
- `validate_trained_candidate()` runtime contract enforcement (cascade and parallel symmetric)
- LSTM cache invariant note from `governance/G1_ADDENDUM_CACHE_CONSISTENCY.md`
- `test_parallel_vs_cascade_artifact_equivalence` test

These refinements remain attached to the paused G2 plan. Do not apply them while G2 is paused; resume them only if the existing parallel/cascade architecture remains the governed target.

##### G3 Reconciliation Queue

Classification: RECONCILIATION (not new gap). Items where two or more existing implementations disagree about a contract, identified during G1 investigation. To be resolved as part of G3 (governed path contract unification).

- **G3-R1: Active validator vs runtime fallback completeness mismatch.** `verify_active_models.py:100-152` enforces one definition of "complete active bundle" (strict, all artifacts required). `ml_predict.py:1291-1294` enforces a different definition (tolerates missing meta with fallback). The two checks disagree on what counts as a valid active model. Invariants violated: I-01 (no silent degradation), I-05 (train-serve feature identity), I-15 (tuple health before trade impact). Status: PENDING. Resolve in G3.

- **G3-R2: `promotion_decision` field is non-authoritative.** `training_cache.py:980, 1029` writes a `promotion_decision` field into candidate manifests. No code path consumes this field as binding for promotion decisions. The field exists, implies authority, but is informational only. Invariants violated: I-02 (single promotion authority), I-14 (attributable change). Status: PENDING. Resolve in G3 by either removing the field or wiring it as authoritative.

- **G3-R3: Lineage horizon mismatch blocks governed evaluation.** Governed evaluation pass fails with `EvaluationLineageError` when manifest horizon does not match expected horizon (observed: manifest `'1c'` vs expected `'5c'`). Source: `arch_competition/lineage.py:29-87` and `arch_competition/eval_runner.py:229-236`. Consequence: `models/arch_competition/` does not exist on this installation because the governed evaluation pass has never successfully produced output. This is why peer-competitor evaluation is not currently operational. Invariants violated: I-10 (reproducible training identity), I-11 (evaluation integrity). Status: PENDING, hard blocker for governed evaluation. Resolve in G3 — unblocks the entire downstream G3-G5 chain.

#### Workstream 2: Infrastructure Governance

Goal: runtime guarantees, system integrity controls, failure containment.

First-class governance gaps. Not secondary, not supporting work. Created from V3 conformance audit findings that did not fit into the model lifecycle phase plan.

| Item | Invariant | Audit row | Urgency | Status |
|------|-----------|-----------|---------|--------|
| INF-1 | I-17 deterministic inference | DOES_NOT_CONFORM_NEW_GAP | HIGH | PENDING |
| INF-2 | I-19 clock synchronization health | DOES_NOT_CONFORM_NEW_GAP | HIGH | PENDING |
| INF-3 | I-20 dependency pinning in serving path | DOES_NOT_CONFORM_NEW_GAP | HIGH | PENDING |
| INF-4 | §14.6 kill switch tri-level halt control | DOES_NOT_CONFORM_NEW_GAP | HIGH | PENDING |

Other 11 NEW_GAP rows (medium urgency) are listed in `governance/V3_CONFORMANCE_AUDIT.md` and will be folded into future phase planning outside Workstream 2 scope (see `governance/PHASE_PLAN_INFRASTRUCTURE.md` §14).

Workstream 2 phase plans: **ACTIVE** — `governance/PHASE_PLAN_INFRASTRUCTURE.md` (INF-1–INF-4 execution, proof, closure, governance events), `governance/PHASE_PLAN_TARGET_STATE.md` (strategic P0–P7 target state and gap map), and reviewer index `governance/INFRASTRUCTURE_GOVERNANCE_LOCK_PACKAGE.md`. Implementation in this workstream must follow those documents per the working discipline (no code without a phase plan).

## GitHub backup state — local-vs-remote-vs-main

**Reality:** operator runs from local launch folder; GitHub is backup only (no other puller). **What belongs in Git vs on disk:** [`docs/host/BACKUP_AND_MIRROR.md`](docs/host/BACKUP_AND_MIRROR.md). **Env template:** [`.env.example`](.env.example) → local `.env` (never commit).

**Current truth (2026-07-08):** all work lands on **`main`**; local `main` == `origin/main` == tip **`5869081`**. The 2026-05-21 feature-branch state (local/origin `feature/institutional-key-levels` @ `e8512be`, origin/main frozen @ `4b8ba2d`) is **HISTORICAL** — main became canonical and carries the full program. Every mission pushes same-day with the four required status checks (Objective Audit / Pytest Full Suite / Hardening Gates / Schwab CSV First Guard) green per commit.

- [x] **Main merge** — RESOLVED: main is canonical (July 2026 program lands directly on main with per-commit 4-gate CI).

**Rule going forward:** every commit is pushed to origin same day. Local-only commits = single point of failure.
